    CONSTRAINT "CK_GrandTotal" CHECK ("GrandTotal" >= 0),

INNER JOIN "DiningSessions" AS d ON t."DiningSessionId" = d."Id"
INNER JOIN "RestaurantTables" AS r ON d."TableId" = r."Id"
LEFT JOIN "OrderItems" AS o0 ON t."Id" = o0."OrderId"
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT d0."Id", d0."DiningSessionId", d0."LinkedAt", d0."TableId", r0."Id" AS "Id0", r0."AssignedWaiterId", r0."Capacity", r0."CreatedAt", r0."IsDeleted", r0."QrIdentifier", r0."Status", r0."TableNumber"
    FROM "DiningSessionTables" AS d0
    INNER JOIN "RestaurantTables" AS r0 ON d0."TableId" = r0."Id"
) AS t0 ON d."Id" = t0."DiningSessionId"
ORDER BY t."PlacedAt" DESC, t."Id", d."Id", r."Id", o0."Id", t0."Id"
